# Truto

> The integration platform for mid-market and enterprise companies. Unified APIs, customizable connectors, and world-class support.

Truto provides a unified API platform for SaaS integrations. This index helps LLMs and AI agents discover every page on https://truto.one, with links that respond to `Accept: text/markdown` (or you can append `.md` to any URL) for clean, normalized markdown.

- Site: https://truto.one
- Blog: https://truto.one/blog
- Blog feed: https://truto.one/blog/feed.xml

## Site pages

- [Pricing — Truto](https://truto.one/pricing/): Get insurance for your integrations. Unlimited connections, API calls, and companies. Starting at $999/connector/year.
- [Integrations — Truto](https://truto.one/integrations/): Natively integrate 650+ apps across 50+ categories. Search our full integration catalog.
- [Customers — Truto](https://truto.one/customers/): Learn how startups and enterprises use Truto to solve problems, save time, and scale. From bootstrapped teams to venture-funded companies.
- [Google Integration API — Skip CASA Assessment | Truto](https://truto.one/google/): Access Gmail, Calendar, Contacts, and Drive APIs without the $15K–$75K CASA assessment. Truto's pre-verified Google OAuth gives you instant access at $10 per connection. A better Nylas alternative.
- [Truto — The Integration Platform That Has Your Back](https://truto.one/): Unified APIs, customizable connectors, and world-class support. Stop building integrations. Start shipping product.
- [Get a 30-day Truto sandbox](https://truto.one/sandbox/): Get a 30-day Truto sandbox to test unified APIs and integrations. Pick your apps, submit a work email, and start building — usually ready within one business day. No credit card required.
- [MCP Servers — Truto](https://truto.one/mcp/): Deploy ready-to-use MCP servers for seamless LLM integration with your APIs and tools. Control access, secure your data, and unlock the full potential of AI.
- [Truto CLI — Truto](https://truto.one/cli/): Manage your entire integration platform from the terminal. Export, batch, diff, and pipe data across 650+ integrations with a single CLI.
- [Merge + Truto — Complete Your Integration Stack](https://truto.one/integration-stack/merge/): Keep Merge for HRIS, ATS, CRM. Add Truto for 200+ connectors across 25+ new categories.
- [Complete Your Integration Stack — Truto](https://truto.one/integration-stack/): Keep your current integration provider for what they do best. Add Truto for everything else.
- [Finch + Truto — Complete Your Integration Stack](https://truto.one/integration-stack/finch/): Keep Finch for HRIS & Payroll. Add Truto for File Storage, Ticketing, Accounting and 25+ other categories.
- [Kombo + Truto — Complete Your Integration Stack](https://truto.one/integration-stack/kombo/): Keep Kombo for HRIS & ATS. Add Truto for CRM, Knowledge-base, Marketing Automation and 25+ other categories.

## Blog categories

- [Product Updates](https://truto.one/blog/product-updates/): New features, launches, and improvements to the Truto platform (30 posts)
- [Engineering](https://truto.one/blog/engineering/): Deep dives into API architecture, integration patterns, and engineering best practices (287 posts)
- [Guides](https://truto.one/blog/guides/): Step-by-step tutorials and practical how-to articles (286 posts)
- [Security](https://truto.one/blog/security/): Security practices, compliance, and trust at Truto (61 posts)
- [By Example](https://truto.one/blog/by-example/): Real-world integration walkthroughs and case studies (78 posts)
- [AI & Agents](https://truto.one/blog/ai-agents/): MCP, LLM connectors, and AI agents connecting to APIs — Claude, ChatGPT, and beyond (1156 posts)
- [General](https://truto.one/blog/general/): Industry insights, opinions, and broader tech topics (214 posts)

## Blog authors

- [Nachi Raman](https://truto.one/blog/nachi/): Co-founder & CEO at Truto. Entrepreneur driven by the challenge of building businesses from the ground up and scaling them to significant outcomes. Works closely with customers to understand their real needs and ensures exceptional products reach and serve customers worldwide. (283 posts)
- [Roopendra Talekar](https://truto.one/blog/roopendra/): Co-founder at Truto. An engineer's engineer who finds genuine delight in making complex systems feel effortless. Leads the technical architecture, ensuring that every API call, every migration, and every data sync runs like a well-oiled machine. (282 posts)
- [Uday Gajavalli](https://truto.one/blog/uday/): Uday builds with customers in the driver's seat. He loves jumping on calls, spotting the real pain behind the feature request, and turning that insight into a product that feels obvious in hindsight. He loves tight feedback loops, clean experiences, and systems that quietly do the right thing. From first click to production scale, he's all about making customers feel supported, successful, and genuinely delighted. (270 posts)
- [Sidharth Verma](https://truto.one/blog/sidharth/): Sidharth is a software engineer and product enthusiast who brings a unique blend of technical expertise and user-centric thinking to Truto. He's passionate about building intuitive, scalable solutions that empower businesses to thrive in the digital age. (273 posts)
- [Yuvraj Muley](https://truto.one/blog/yuvraj/): Yuvraj is a software engineer who enjoys working in fast-paced teams and turning messy or unclear requirements into reliable, production-ready software. He is comfortable across the stack and works with TypeScript, Java, and DevOps depending on what the problem needs. (281 posts)
- [Riya Sethi](https://truto.one/blog/riya/): Senior Software Engineer at Truto. She makes Truto's builder experience sharper end to end. She ships CLI tooling and in-product workflows that help teams author integrations faster. She loves tight feedback loops and tooling that does the careful checking so builders don't have to hold it all in their heads. She's happiest when a teammate can ship an integration confidently without a late-night prod surprise. (183 posts)
- [Nidhi KN](https://truto.one/blog/nidhi/): Product and content at Truto. (18 posts)
